## 2026-05-31T17-49 - P0-14 GPU Grass Carpet Baseline

Item: `14. GPU-instanced living grass ground-carpet under the cards (RenderMeshIndirect + GPU cull)` (`NEEDS-TOM`).

What changed: added a conservative dense grass carpet under the Central Plaza foliage/card ground patch. The runtime component `FastVsHd2dGpuGrassCarpet` generates deterministic blade data into a `GraphicsBuffer`, renders a 3-triangle tapered blade mesh through `Graphics.RenderMeshIndirect`, and uses `FastVS_GpuGrassCull.compute` to write a frustum+distance culled visible-blade buffer before copying the append count into the indirect-args buffer. Current and past review carpets each target `50000` source blades and expose blade count, area, tint, wind, pixel-snap, and cull distance as serialized data for Tom to retune without code.

## 2026-06-01T04-12 - P1-29 Triplanar Projection

Item: `29. Triplanar projection for cliff faces, slopes, and steep diorama edges` (`auto-safe`).

What changed: extended `FastVS_SurfaceRampLit` with a steep-normal-only triplanar path for the rock/splat-A layer. The shader now exposes `_UseSplatATriplanar`, `_SplatATriplanarScale`, `_SplatATriplanarBlendStart`, and `_SplatATriplanarBlendEnd`; it samples the rock map from world-space `zy/xz/xy` axes, blends by `pow(abs(normalWS), 4)`, and masks the result to vertical/steep surfaces so flat grass/path/top surfaces keep the existing top-down UV and P1-28 height-aware splat behavior. The vertex-splat demo material enables this path at scale `1.35`, steep blend `0.58/0.82`. A small review mesh `Assets/Art/Meshes/FastVS/FastVS_HD2D_P1_TriplanarCliffProjectionDemo.asset` was added with a deliberately UV-compressed vertical rock face and a grass/rock top lip to prove the cliff case without changing unrelated scene assets.

## 2026-06-01T05-52 - P1-32 Interactive Grass Bend Map

Item: `32. Interactive grass bend / trample via a top-down bend-map RenderTexture` (`auto-safe`).

What changed: upgraded the existing P0-15 vegetation wind manager from a static neutral `_BendMap` publisher to a live interactive bend-map path. `FastVsHd2dVegetationWindManager` now owns a 128x128 ARGB RenderTexture, CPU staging texture, decay loop, player/portal/review trampler splats, `_BendMapWorldRect`, and `_BendMapStrength` globals. The conservative candidate is the preferred Tom-facing replacement because it smooths the frame and avoids a hard oval; final intensity, smoothness, rounded mode, color tint, and whether to relax existing validation remain Tom decisions.

Recommendation: keep the profile, validation guards, deterministic A/B capture, runtime proof, and R2 evidence. Leave the shared runtime Vignette at the existing locked value and `finalDioramaVignetteApproved=false`; Tom should approve whether to move to the conservative `0.30 / 0.58 / rounded on` candidate after checking corner gameplay readability in the approved camera/grade.
